{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c77baf6f2380d9f43e3bb8e8e21616","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_nbrjidg77lt8_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/patches/README.md","content":"# Apache Druid JavaScript Filter Security Vulnerability Patch\n\n## Vulnerability Description\n\nCVE-2021-XXXXX: Apache Druid version 0.20.0 contains a security vulnerability that allows authenticated attackers to execute arbitrary code on the server through malicious JavaScript payloads.\n\n### Exploit\n\nThe vulnerability can be exploited by sending a POST request to `/druid/indexer/v1/sampler` with a malicious payload:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"index\",\n  \"spec\": {\n    \"dataSchema\": {\n      \"transformSpec\": {\n        \"filter\": {\n          \"type\": \"javascript\",\n          \"function\": \"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('malicious_command');}\",\n          \"\": {\n            \"enabled\": true\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\nThe empty key `\"\"` can bypass JavaScript security settings to allow arbitrary code execution.\n\n### Root Cause\n\nThe vulnerability is caused by a Jackson deserialization attack. When Jackson deserializes the `JavaScriptDimFilter` object from JSON, it accepts the empty key `\"\"` and uses it to inject the `enabled: true` setting, bypassing the JavaScript security check.\n\n## Fix\n\nThis patch adds the following security measures:\n\n1. **Jackson Configuration**: Added `@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = false)` annotation to the `JavaScriptDimFilter` class to prevent Jackson from accepting unknown properties.\n\n2. **Input Validation**: Added validation in the `JavaScriptDimFilter` constructor to reject empty dimension strings.\n\n### Changes Made\n\n- Modified `processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/query/filter/JavaScriptDimFilter.java`:\n  - Added import for `@JsonIgnoreProperties`\n  - Added `@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = false)` annotation to the class\n  - Added validation to reject empty dimension strings in the constructor\n\n### Impact\n\nThis fix prevents the exploitation of the vulnerability by:\n- Blocking JSON payloads with empty keys\n- Rejecting empty dimension strings\n- Ensuring that only properly configured JavaScript filters are accepted\n\n### Testing\n\nAfter applying the patch, verify that:\n1. Legitimate JavaScript filters continue to work\n2. Malicious payloads with empty keys are rejected\n3. The server properly validates all filter configurations\n\n### References\n\n- Apache Druid Security Advisory\n- CVE-2021-XXXXX\n- Jackson Security Documentation\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":640,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1502,"cache_read_input_tokens":49892},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c901edab-aa78-46c7-ab1a-5ad52a2f51de","uuid":"55318c8a-66f5-4b2f-b90a-5cd09309e459"}
